# 인물 얼굴 더 넣기 (11/24) · 그림 103장

한 판에 내 나라 사람이 약 110명인데 얼굴은 62장뿐이라 46명이 다른 사람과 얼굴이 같아요. 아래 그림이 들어오면 **모든 인물 얼굴이 달라지고**, 부관은 **젊은 얼굴**, 수행비서는 **20대 여성 한 사람**으로 고정돼요.

## 한눈 표

| 순서 | 무엇 | 그림 | 채팅창 | 첨부(첫 그림만) | 저장 이름 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 수행비서(나라마다 20대 여성 1명) | 13장(1명씩) | 창 1개 | ref-face-spot.png · ref-young-1.webp · ref-young-2.webp | `secretary-kr.png` 등 |
| 2 | 젊은 부관(20~30대) 400명 | 25장(4×4 = 16명) | 창 1개 | ref-face-spot.png · ref-young-1.webp · ref-young-2.webp | `sheet-kr-y1.png` 등 |
| 3 | 장관·참모·관료(40~70대) 1040명 | 65장(4×4 = 16명) | 나라마다 창 1개(13개) | ref-face-spot.png · leader-<나라>.webp | `sheet-kr-9.png` ~ `-13.png` |

- 참고 그림은 `guides/face-more-ref.zip` 하나에 모두 있어요.
- 순서: **채팅창 열기 → 첨부 → 틀 명령문 → 장 명령문 하나씩 → 적힌 이름으로 저장 → zip으로 올리기**.
- **한국 것부터** 하면 좋아요(수행비서 1장 + 부관 1장 + 장관급 5장 = 7장이면 한국 판은 겹침이 사라져요). 나라별로 따로 올려도 돼요.
- 4×4가 뭉개지면 그 장만 다시 만들거나 말씀해 주세요. 3×3 명령문으로 바꿔 드릴게요.

---

## 창 1 · 수행비서 13장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + ref-young-1.webp + ref-young-2.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) showing ONE single person (not a grid). no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message. This chat is for the leader's personal secretaries: each is a woman in her twenties, smart and trustworthy.
```

### secretary-kr.png · 대한민국 수행비서 윤서아 (여 27세)
```
27-year-old South Korean (ethnic Korean) woman, the national leader's personal secretary, neat low ponytail, soft oval face, gentle confident smile, a charcoal skirt suit and white blouse, a slim ID lanyard, holding a dark tablet at chest height. Background: blurred government office corridor at night. File name: save this image as secretary-kr.png
```

## 창 2 · 젊은 부관 25장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + ref-young-1.webp + ref-young-2.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message. This chat is for young military aides (adjutants) in their twenties and thirties.
```

## 창 3 · 대한민국 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-kr.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 4 · 북한 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-kp.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 5 · 일본 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-jp.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 8 · 영국 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-uk.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 9 · 독일 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-de.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 12 · 인도 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-in.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 13 · 이란 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-ir.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 14 · 이스라엘 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-il.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```

## 창 15 · 튀르키예 장관급 5장 (첨부: ref-face-spot.png + leader-tr.webp)

틀 명령문(첫 그림 전에 한 번, 지난번 그 나라 창을 이어 쓰면 생략):
```
I am making character portraits for a near-future modern military and politics strategy game. The attached images show where a face appears in my game (a small square on a dark console UI) and portraits I already made; match their art style and painting quality exactly. Every image in this chat follows these fixed rules: ONE square 1:1 image (1024x1024 or larger) that is a contact sheet of separate portraits in an exact equal 4x4 grid (16 cells) unless I say otherwise, every cell the same size, separated by a thin straight dark gap. Cells are numbered left-to-right, top-to-bottom;. no frames, no text, no numbers, no letters, no logos, no flags, no real insignia anywhere. Each portrait: semi-realistic digital painting (realistic proportions, skin and fabric texture, like a high-quality modern mobile strategy game; not anime, not cartoon, not a photograph); an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL person who must NOT resemble any real person, politician or celebrity; every person clearly different from the others (different face shape, nose, eyes, hair, build); head and upper chest, body turned about 15 degrees, face centred with eyes at about 40 percent from the top; soft cinematic key light from the top-left with a gentle warm rim light; background heavily blurred and darkened toward the edges. I will send one description at a time; make exactly ONE image per message.
```
